# INSTRUCTION-VERIFY reference trace - `ARGUMENT`

- Program: INSTRUCTION-B (204384B, 83.11.01), started with `0!`, command `ARGUMENT`
- Emulator: ND110CPU (ND-110, RASK microcode `ND-110-RASK.LISTING.TXT`)
- All values OCTAL. Macro registers are the CURRENT level's bank; `PIL` marks the level.
- Watched set: A D T X B L P STS R1 R2 R3 R4 R5 R6 R7 Q F GPR LC (R1-R7 = current-level scratch, Q/F = Am2901 Q register / ALU output latch, GPR = general purpose shift register, LC = loop counter)
- One `###` section per macro instruction: `addr : opcode` + full register state at fetch.
- Micro rows: `csar | symbol | changed-registers | microcode source`. Only registers that changed during that microinstruction are shown.
- The `symbol` column comes from `ND-110-RASK.SYMBOLS.TXT` - exact label, or `LABEL+n` (octal offset from the nearest preceding label). These label names are the PORTABLE cross-reference for the ND-120 DELILAH microcode, whose control-store ADDRESSES differ but whose routine labels are (mostly) the same.
- Detailed capture is ARMED at the first macro instruction that executes on a TEST level (PIL 1-9) OR is fetched from the test-framework/test-code region (022000-046777 octal) - whichever happens first - and then records the next 400 macro instructions across all levels (test code, the framework's check code, and any clock-level interrupts that interleave). The command-dispatch/console preamble (047000+) is skipped (count reported in the summary).
- Capture stops at END OF TEST / error / cap.
